CORPORATE PROFILE
公司概況
China Foods Limited (“China Foods” or the “Company” and together with its
subsidiaries, the “Group”), a subsidiary of COFCO Corporation (“COFCO”) and the
only focused beverage platform of COFCO, is listed on the main board of The Stock
Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (Stock Code: 506).


The Company mainly manufactures, distributes, markets and sells the Coca-
Cola series products at nineteen provincial-level administrative regions in the PRC
through COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited (“COFCO Coca-Cola”), a 65%-owned
joint-venture with The Coca-Cola Company. COFCO Coca-Cola offers twenty-five
brands of products in ten major types of beverages which include sparkling drink,
juice, water, milk drink, energy drink, tea, coffee, functional nutrition drink, sports
drink and plant-based protein drink.


中國食品有限公司（簡稱「中國食品」或「本公司」，連同其附屬公司統稱「本集團」）
為中糧集團有限公司（「中糧」）的附屬公司及中糧唯一的專業化飲料業務平台，並
於香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市（股份代號：506）。


本公司目前主要通過與可口可樂公司合作成立並持有65%權益的中糧可口可樂飲
料有限公司（簡稱「中糧可口可樂」）在國內19個省級行政區域從事生產、配送、推
廣 和 銷 售 可 口 可 樂 系 列 產 品，向 消 費 者 提 供 包 括 汽 水、果 汁、水、乳 飲 料、能 量
飲 料、茶、咖 啡、功 能 型 營 養 素 飲 料、運 動 飲 料 及 植 物 蛋 白 飲 料 等10大 品 類、25
個品牌的產品。

                                                                  MAJOR EVENTS IN 2024
                                                                        2024 年大事記



 Groundbreaking Ceremony of Shaanxi New Plant
 陝西新廠開工儀式




On 30 January 2024, the construction of the new plant of COFCO Coca-Cola in Shaanxi was officially
commenced. Chairman of COFCO Corporation, Lu Jun, Chief Accountant, Su Jian, Chairman and CEO of
The Coca-Cola Company, James Quincey, and leaders from the Xi’an municipal government and relevant
departments attended and laid the foundation for the new plant.

2024年1月30日，中糧可口可樂陝西新廠正式開工。中糧集團董事長呂軍、總會計師粟健，可口可樂公司董
事長兼CEO詹鯤杰（James Quincey）、西安市政府及相關部門領導出席並為新廠奠基。

      Chairman of COFCO Corporation, Lu Jun Conducted a Research on China Foods’ Business
      中糧集團呂軍董事長調研中國食品業務




    On 30 January 2024, Chairman of COFCO Corporation, Lu Jun conducted a market survey of COFCO Coca-
    Cola, acknowledging the market execution and fully affirming the expansion of new businesses such as smart
    retail.

    2024年1月30日，中糧集團董事長呂軍調研中糧可口可樂市場，對於市場執行給與認可，對於智慧零售等新
    業務拓展表示充分肯定。

 Core Management Team of The Coca-Cola Company Conducted Multiple Research Visits to market of
 China Foods
 可口可樂公司核心管理團隊多次調研中國食品市場




James Quincey, Global Chairman and CEO of Coca-Cola, John Murphy, Global President and CFO of Coca-
Cola, Henrique Braun, Global Executive Vice President and CEO of Coca-Cola, Nancy Quan, Global Senior
Vice President and Chief Technical Innovation Officer of Coca-Cola, Karina Espinel, Global President of
R&D Innovation of Coca-Cola, and other senior executives visited China to research COFCO Coca-Cola, fully
recognizing the potential of the Chinese market and the business development of COFCO Coca-Cola.

可口可樂全球董事長兼首席執行官詹鯤杰（James Quincey）、可口可樂全球總裁兼首席財務官莊慕斐（John
Murphy）、可口可樂全球執行副總裁兼首席執行官柏瑞凱(Henrique Braun)，可口可樂全球高級副總裁兼首
席技術創新官Nancy Quan、可口可樂全球研發創新總裁Karina Espinel等高管均來華調研中糧可口可樂，充
分認可中國市場潛力及中糧可口可樂業務發展。




                                                                                中 國 食 品 有 限公司 年 報 2024        5
    MAJOR EVENTS IN 2024 2 0 2 4 年 大 事 記




      Clean Drinking Water in 24 Hours
      淨水24小時




    In 2024, COFCO Coca-Cola activated the “Clean Drinking Water in 24 Hours” emergency drinking water
    rescue mechanism 30 times, delivering 924,496 bottles of drinking water to regions such as Guizhou, Hunan,
    Sichuan and Shandong, effectively alleviating the water needs of disaster-stricken areas. With the motto “Water
    Delivered, Care Delivered”, we fulfilled the promise of “Clean Drinking Water in 24 Hours” through concrete
    actions, providing timely assistance to every region in need.

    2024年，中 糧 可 口 可 樂 先 後30次 啓 動「淨 水24小 時」應 急 飲 用 水 救 援 機 制，向 貴 州、湖 南、四 川、山 東 等 地
    區運送924,496瓶飲用水，有效緩解受災地區用水需求，「水到、心到」我們以實際行動踐行「淨水24小時」的
    承諾，為每一個需要幫助的地區及時提供援助。




6   CHINA FOODS LIMITED ANNUAL REPORT 2024
                                                                         MAJOR EVENTS IN 2024 2 0 2 4 年 大 事 記




  Launch Meeting of Specialized Pilot Operation for Production and Sales in the Beijing-Tianjin-Hebei
  Region
  京津冀產銷職能專業化試點運營啓動會




On 25 December 2024, COFCO Coca-Cola's specialized operation for production and sales in the Beijing-
Tianjin-Hebei region was officially commenced. This aimed to "meet customer business needs, enhance
efficiency, and improve profitability", by innovating business models and operational mechanisms, seeking new
opportunities for quality improvement and efficiency enhancement for China Foods.

2024年12月25日，中糧可口可樂京津冀產銷專業化運營正式啓動，該項目以「滿足客戶業務需求，提升效率、
提高效益」為目標，創新業務模式和運營機制，為中國食品尋找新的提質增效機會。




                                                                                   中 國 食 品 有 限公司 年 報 2024       7
    MAJOR EVENTS IN 2024 2 0 2 4 年 大 事 記




      Work in 2024 Was Highly Recognized by External Parties
      2024年各項工作受到外界高度認可




    In 2024, various operating units of China Foods received the "National and Provincial-Level" honorary awards
    63 times. All plants were awarded the annual "Lucid Waters and Lush Mountains Cup（綠水青山杯）" title for
    excellent water and energy saving enterprises by the Beverage Industry Association. Currently, eight plants are
    recognized as national-level green plants, seven as provincial-level green plants, and two as provincial-level
    green supply chain enterprises.

    2024年年度中國食品各經營單位63次獲評「國家級、省部級」榮譽獎項，全部工廠均獲得飲料行業協會年度「綠
    水青山杯」節水節能優秀企業稱號，目前8家工廠為國家級綠色工廠，7家工廠為省級綠色工廠，2家工廠為省
    級綠色供應鏈企業。

                                                           STATEMENT FROM MANAGING DIRECTOR 董 事 總 經 理 函 件




2024: FORGING NEW HEIGHTS WITH                                      2024年：以重塑鑄就新高峰
RESHAPING
In 2024, in the face of a complex and volatile economic             2024年，面 對 複 雜 多 變 的 經 濟 環 境 與 行
environment and industry competition, China Foods proactively       業競爭，中國食品主動出擊，啓動重塑之
embarked on a journey of transformation, comprehensively            旅，全面覆蓋產品品類、營銷網絡、價值
covering the entire business process including product              鏈、供應平台等業務全流程。
categories, marketing networks, value chains and supply
platforms.

In terms of product category reshaping, the Company                 在產品品類重塑方面，公司聚焦汽水、果
focused on two core categories: sparkling drinks and juices,        汁兩大核心品類，鞏固市場領先地位，同
consolidating its market-leading position while accelerating the    時加速拓展即飲茶和即飲咖啡兩大新興
expansion of two emerging categories: ready-to-drink tea and        品 類。其 中，汽 水 通 過「有 糖 無 糖 雙 輪 驅
ready-to-drink coffee. Among them, sparkling drinks maintained      動」，市 場 份 額 繼 續 保 持 品 類 絕 對 優 勢 地
an absolute advantage in market share in the category through       位；果汁聚焦即飲包裝，擴大飲用消費，
the “dual drives of sugared and sugar-free options”; while juices   在低濃度果汁市場持續領跑。陽光品牌即
focused on ready-to-drink packaging, expanding consumption,         飲茶鋪貨率提升15%；即飲咖啡聚焦重點
and continued to lead in the low-concentration juice market.        區域，通過COSTA聯名及新品絲絨系列，
The distribution rate of the Sunshine brand ready-to-drink tea      不斷強化品牌影響；能量飲料推出獵獸新
increased 15%; while ready-to-drink coffee concentrated on          品，利用全新的數字化消費者營銷拓展市
key areas to continuously strengthen brand influence through        場。
COSTA co-branding and the new Velvet series; energy drinks
launched the new Predator product, using innovative digital
consumer marketing to expand the market.

In terms of reshaping the marketing network, we strove to           在營銷網絡重塑方面，力圖通過高效執行
achieve precise reach through efficient execution. The Company      實現精準觸達。公司聚焦渠道深化，直營
focused on channel deepening, with the proportion of direct         客戶佔比提升至46%，餐飲渠道收入同比
customers increasing to 46%, revenue from the catering              增 長132%，鄉 鎮 市 場 覆 蓋 率 擴 大 至280
channel growing by 132% year-on-year, and the coverage rate         個城市。與此同時，公司聚焦終端優化，
of township markets expanding to 280 cities. At the same time,      確 保 售 點 執 行 和 資 源 投 入 產 出。市 場 鋪
the Company focused on terminal optimization to ensure point-       貨率提升20%，重點區域冰凍化終端增長
of-sale execution and resource investment returns. The market       35%，有效拉動消費場景滲透。
distribution rate increased by 20%, and the growth of frozen

                                      CHAIRMAN OF THE BOARD AND NON-EXECUTIVE DIRECTOR
                                      董事會主席兼非執行董事
                                      MR. QING LIJUN 慶立軍先生




MR. QING LIJUN
Mr. Qing, 55, was appointed the chairman of the board of the Company in September 2022 and has been as an
executive director and the managing director of the Company since January 2020. On 6 January 2025, Mr. Qing has
tendered his resignation as the managing director and ceased to act as the chairman of the executive committee of
the board, and he has been re-designated from an executive director to a non-executive director of the Company.
Mr. Qing is currently the deputy general manager of COFCO Corporation, a non-executive director and the chairman
of the board of directors of China Mengniu Dairy Company Limited (stock code: 2319), the chairman of board of
directors of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited, a 65%-owned subsidiary of the Company. Mr. Qing joined COFCO
Corporation and/or its subsidiaries in July 1993 and has held various positions including the chief executive officer
of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited, the director of strategy department of COFCO Corporation during the period
from July 2016 to March 2019. Mr. Qing was the deputy general manager of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited
during the period from December 2007 to June 2016. Mr. Qing has concurrently served as the strategy planning
director of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited during the period April 2004 to March 2014. During the period
from April 2004 to June 2016, Mr. Qing has also concurrently served various positions including the director of the
public relations department and the director of the finance department of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited, the
general manager of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages (Shandong) Limited and the general manager of COFCO Coca-Cola
Beverages (Beijing) Limited. Mr. Qing has served as the deputy general manager of Jilin COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages
Limited during the period from June 2002 to March 2004 and a sales operation manager of COFCO Coca-Cola
Beverages Limited during the period from April 2000 to May 2002.

Mr. Qing graduated from Renmin University of China and Guanghua School of Business Management of Peking University
with a Bachelor’s degree in Economics and a Master’s degree in Business Administration, respectively. Mr. Qing has extensive
experience in beverage production, marketing, strategy planning, finance, public relations and general management.

MR. MOK WAI BUN, BEN
Mr. Mok, 76, was appointed as an independent non-executive director of the Company in August 2017. Mr.

of Swire Beverages Limited in China.

extensive experience in beverage production, marketing and general management.

     MS. LEUNG KA LAI, ADA, SBS
     Ms. Leung, age 64, was appointed as an independent non-executive director of the Company in June 2022.
     Ms. Leung is currently a member of Standing Commission on Civil Service Salaries and Conditions of Service
     of the HKSAR Government. She joined the HKSAR Government in September 1983 and has served in the
     civil service for 35 years until her retirement in 2019. Ms. Leung joined the Intellectual Property Department
     (IPD) of the HKSAR Government as a senior solicitor in 1995 and was the director of Intellectual Property
     and Registrar of Trade Marks, Patents and Designs since May 2014 until her retirement in March 2019.
     Prior to joining IPD, Ms. Leung was a senior crown counsel with the then Attorney General’s Chambers (now
     Department of Justice). She had worked with both the Litigation Unit and the Advisory Unit in the Civil Division.
     Ms. Leung was also a part-time Lecturer, Faculty of Law, of University of Hong Kong during the period from
     October 2020 to August 2021.
     Ms. Leung was appointed as an Official Justice of Peace by the HKSAR Government since 2012 and until
     February 2020. She was awarded the Silver Bauhinia Star (SBS) by the HKSAR Government in 2018.
     Ms. Leung has over 20 years professional experience in IP law practice as well as financial and staff
     management, management of outsourcing contracts as well as planning and implementing promotion and
     public education programmes in the public sector. She also has extensive experience in formulation of policy,
     law and practice in different areas of intellectual property as well as building and maintaining network with
     local and international stakeholders, including the legal profession, intellectual property practitioners, trade
     and business organisations, overseas and Mainland counterparts and inter-governmental organisations. She
     has also represented Hong Kong, China in different international conferences and meetings in formulating
     IP policies at the international level. She also has experience in civil advisory, civil litigation and criminal
     prosecution with the then Attorney General’s Chambers (now Department of Justice).
     Ms. Leung holds a Bachelor Degree in Science from the University of Hong Kong. She then obtained a
     Government Legal Training Scholarship and studied law in the University of Buckingham in England. She was
     called to the Bar in England and in Hong Kong.

Risk Management and Internal Control                                   風險管理及內部監控
The Board acknowledges its responsibility for maintaining              董事會確認其對維持適合及有效的風險
appropriate and effective risk management and internal control         管理和內部監控制度承擔責任，並通過董
systems, and for reviewing the effectiveness of these systems          事會審核委員會檢討這些系統的有效性。
through the audit committee of the Board.

Sound risk management and internal control systems are based           健全的風險管理和內部監控體系是基於
on the Company’s good business ethics and culture, the quality         本公司良好的商業道德與文化、員工的素
and capabilities of its employees, the direction of the Board,         質與能力、董事會的方向以及管理層的效
and also the efficiency and capabilities of management.                率與能力。

Management of the Company, under the Board supervision, is             本公司的管理層在董事會的監督下，負責
responsible for the design, implementation and monitoring of           風險管理和內部監控系統的設計，實施和
the risk management and internal control systems.                      監督。

The Group has established a sound and comprehensive risk               本集團已經建立起完善的全面風險管理
management and internal control system, formed a systematic            和內部監控體系，形成了科學有效的職責
and effective system on segregation of duty and three lines of         分工體系，建立了風險管理三道防線，即
defense in risk management, with each of the business units as         業務部門為第一道防線；各級業務監督檢
the first line of defense; the business supervision and inspection     查部門為第二道防線；內審部和紀委辦公
departments at all levels as the second line of defense; and           室為第三道防線。
internal audit department and disciplinary inspection office as
the third line of defense.

     measures. These risks mainly cover market competition, safe           市場競爭、安全生產、成本壓力和中美關

    China Foods Limited (the “Company”) is a limited liability             中 國 食 品 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）為

    of the Company is located at Clarendon House, 2 Church                 任 公 司。本 公 司 之 註 冊 辦 事 處 位 於
    Street, Hamilton HM 11, Bermuda.                                       Clarendon House, 2 Church Street,

        Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group                當本集團取得附屬公司之控制

        Specifically, income and expenses of a subsidiary                  權，便 停 止 將 該 附 屬 公 司 綜 合

                                                                           日為止。

        Company and to the non-controlling interests. Total                及 非 控 股 權 益。附 屬 公 司 的 全

        to the owners of the Company and to the non-                       有 人 及 非 控 股 權 益，即 使 這 將
        controlling interests even if this results in the non-             導致非控股權益的金額為負數。
        controlling interests having a deficit balance.

        accounting policies.

    3.2 Material accounting policy information                           3.2 重大會計政策信息（續）
        (continued)
        Business combination                                                 業務合併
        A business is an integrated set of activities and                    業 務 為 一 整 組 活 動 及 資 產，包
        assets which includes an input and a substantive                     括一項資源投入及一項實質過
        process that together significantly contribute to the                程，而 兩 者 必 須 對 創 造 產 出 的
        ability to create outputs. The acquired processes                    能 力 有 重 大 貢 獻。倘 收 購 過 程
        are considered substantive if they are critical to                   對繼續生產產出的能力至關重
        the ability to continue producing outputs, including                 要，包 括 具 備 執 行 有 關 過 程 所
        an organised workforce with the necessary skills,                    必 需 的 技 能、知 識 或 經 驗 的 組
        knowledge, or experience to perform the related                      織 勞 動 力，或 對 持 續 生 產 產 出
        processes or they significantly contribute to the                    的 能 力 有 重 大 貢 獻，則 被 認 為
        ability to continue producing outputs and are                        屬 獨 特 或 稀 缺，或 在 無 重 大 成
        considered unique or scarce or cannot be replaced                    本、努 力 或 持 續 生 產 產 出 能 力
        without significant cost, effort, or delay in the ability            出現延遲的情況下不可取代。
        to continue producing outputs.

        Acquisitions of businesses are accounted for                         收 購 業 務 採 用 收 購 法 入 賬。業
        using the acquisition method. The consideration                      務合併所轉讓之代價按公平值
        transferred in a business combination is measured                    計 量，而 計 算 方 法 為 本 集 團 所
        at fair value, which is calculated as the sum of the                 轉 讓 之 資 產、本 集 團 向 被 收 購
        acquisition-date fair values of the assets transferred               方原擁有人產生之負債及本集
        by the Group, liabilities incurred by the Group to                   團為交換被收購方之控制權發
        the former owners of the acquiree and the equity                     行之股權於收購日期之公平值
        interests issued by the Group in exchange for                        之 總 額。收 購 事 項 相 關 成 本 通
        control of the acquiree. Acquisition-related costs are               常於產生時於損益中確認。
        generally recognised in profit or loss as incurred.

        The identifiable assets acquired and liabilities                     所收購之可識別資產及所承擔
        assumed must meet the definitions of an asset and                    之負債必須符合財務報告概念
        a liability in the Conceptual Framework for Financial                框架（「概念框架」）中資產及負
        Reporting (the “Conceptual Framework”) except for                    債 的 定 義，惟 於 香 港 會 計 準 則
        transactions and events within the scope of HKAS                     第37號「撥 備、或 有 負 債 及 或
        37 “Provisions, Contingent Liabilities and Contingent                有資產」或香港（國際財務報告
        Assets” or HK(IFRIC)-Int 21 “Levies”, in which                       詮 釋 委 員 會）－詮 釋 第21號「徵
        the Group applies HKAS 37 or HK(IFRIC)-Int 21                        費」範圍內的交易及事件除外，
        instead of the Conceptual Framework to identify the                  本集團對此應用香港會計準則
        liabilities it has assumed in a business combination.                第37號 或 香 港（國 際 財 務 報 告
        Contingent assets are not recognised.                                詮 釋 委 員 會）－詮 釋 第21號 而
                                                                             非概念框架識別其於業務合併
                                                                             中 承 擔 的 負 債。或 有 資 產 不 獲
                                                                             確認。

                the identifiable assets and liabilities over the cost of         估後的公平值淨額高於投資成
                the investment, after reassessment, is recognised                本，則 會 於 收 購 投 資 期 間 即 時

        Revenue recognition                                                收入確認
        The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a                        於（或就此）完成履約義務時，
        performance obligation is satisfied, i.e. when                     本 集 團 確 認 收 入，即 於 特 定 履
        “control” of the goods or services underlying the                  約義務的相關商品或服務的「控
        particular performance obligation is transferred to                制權」轉讓予客戶時確認。
        the customer.

        A performance obligation represents a good or                     履約義務指不同的商品及服務
        service (or a bundle of goods or services) that is                （或一組商品或服務）或一系列
        distinct or a series of distinct goods or services that           大致相等的不同商品及服務。
        are substantially the same.

        Control is transferred over time and revenue is                    控 制 權 隨 時 間 轉 移，而 倘 滿 足
        recognised over time by reference to the progress                  以 下 其 中 一 項 標 準，則 收 入 乃
        towards complete satisfaction of the relevant                      參照完全滿足相關履約義務的
        performance obligation if one of the following criteria            進展情況而隨時間確認：
        is met:

        •    the customer simultaneously receives and                      •    於本集團履約時，客戶同
             consumes the benefits provided by the Group’s                      時取得並耗用本集團履
             performance as the Group performs;                                 約所提供的利益；

        •    the Group’s performance creates or enhances                   •    本集團之履約產生或強
             an asset that the customer controls as the                         化一項資產，該資產於本
             Group performs; or                                                 集團履約時乃由客戶控
                                                                                制；或

        •    the Group’s performance does not create an                    •    本集團的履約並未產生
             asset with an alternative use to the Group and                     對本集團有替代用途的
             the Group has an enforceable right to payment                      資產，且本集團對迄今已
             for performance completed to date.                                 完成履約之付款具有可
                                                                                執行之權利。

        Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time                否 則，收 入 於 客 戶 獲 得 商 品 或
        when the customer obtains control of the distinct                  服務控制權的時間點確認。
        goods or services.

                For contracts that contain variable consideration,             就 包 含 可 變 代 價 的 合 約（如 以
                such as volume related discount, the Group                     數 量 為 基 礎 的 折 扣）而 言，本
                estimates the amount of consideration to which it              集團可使用(a)期望價值法或(b)
                will be entitled using either (a) the expected value           最可能金額估計其有權獲取的
                method or (b) the most likely amount, depending                代 價 金 額，取 決 於 哪 種 方 法 將
                on which method better predicts the amount of                  能更好的預測本集團有權獲取
                consideration to which the Group will be entitled.             的代價金額。

                The estimated amount of variable consideration is              可變代價的估計金額將計入交
                included in the transaction price only to the extent           易 價 格，並 以 當 可 變 代 價 相 關
                that it is highly probable that such an inclusion will         不 確 定 因 素 隨 後 獲 解 除，該 入
                not result in a significant revenue reversal in the            賬將不會導致重大收入撥回為
                future when the uncertainty associated with the                限。
                variable consideration is subsequently resolved.

                reporting period and the changes in circumstances

        the reporting period.                                              已頒佈之稅率計算。

        and the corresponding tax bases used in the                        差 額 確 認。遞 延 稅 項 負 債 一 般
        computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities            就 所 有 應 課 稅 暫 時 差 額 確 認。
        are generally recognised for all taxable temporary                 遞延稅項資產一般於可能有應

        respectively. Where current tax or deferred tax                       或遞延稅項產生自業務合併的

        accounting for the business combination.

           3.2 Material accounting policy information                        3.2 重大會計政策信息（續）
               (continued)
                Inventories                                                    存貨
                Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net            存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩
                realisable value. Costs of inventories are determined          者 中 之 較 低 者 入 賬。成 本 乃 以
                on a weighted average method. Net realisable value             加 權 平 均 法 計 算。可 變 現 淨 值
                represents the estimated selling price for inventories         代表存貨之估計售價減去估計
                less all estimated costs of completion and costs               完成成本及進行銷售所需之成
                necessary to make the sale. Costs necessary to                 本。進 行 銷 售 所 需 的 成 本 包 括
                make the sale include incremental costs directly               直接歸屬於銷售的增量成本以
                attributable to the sale and non-incremental costs             及本集團進行銷售所必須產生
                which the Group must incur to make the sale.                   的非增量成本。

                Provisions                                                     撥備
                Provisions are recognised when the Group has                   如本集團須就已發生的事件承
                a present obligation (legal or constructive) as a              擔 法 律 或 推 定 現 有 責 任，而 預
                result of a past event, it is probable that the Group          期本集團需要清償責任及可以
                will be required to settle that obligation, and a              作 出 可 靠 估 計 責 任 之 金 額 時，
                reliable estimate can be made of the amount of the             則需確認撥備。
                obligation.

                The amount recognised as a provision is the best               確認撥備之金額為在報告期末
                estimate of the consideration required to settle the           時對清償現有責任所需之代價
                present obligation at the end of the reporting period,         的 最 佳 估 計，並 考 慮 責 任 相 關
                taking into account the risks and uncertainties                之 風 險 及 不 穩 定 因 素。倘 撥 備
                surrounding the obligation. When a provision is                是按估計清償現有責任之現金
                measured using the cash flows estimated to settle              流 確 認 的，其 賬 面 值 即 為 該 現
                the present obligation, its carrying amount is the             金 流 量 之 現 時 價 值（如 金 錢 之
                present value of those cash flows (when the effect of          時間值為重大的）。
                the time value of money is material).

        Financial instruments                                               金融工具
        Financial assets and financial liabilities are                      金融資產及金融負債乃當某集
        recognised when a group entity becomes a party                      團實體成為工具合約條文之訂
        to the contractual provisions of the instrument. All                約 方 時 確 認。所 有 以 一 般 方 式
        regular way purchases or sales of financial assets                  買賣之金融資產均按買賣日期
        are recognised and derecognised on a trade date                     基 準 確 認 及 終 止 確 認。一 般 方
        basis. Regular way purchases or sales are purchases                 式買賣指須於市場規管或慣例
        or sales of financial assets that require delivery                  所設定時限內交付資產之金融
        of assets within the time frame established by                      資產買賣。
        regulation or convention in the market place.

        Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially            除來自根據香港財務報告準則
        measured at fair value except for trade receivables                 第15號「客戶合約收入」     （「香港
        arising from contracts with customers which are                     財務報告準則第15號」）初步計
        initially measured in accordance with HKFRS 15                      量之客戶合約所產生應收貿易
        “Revenue from Contracts with Customers” (“HKFRS                     款 項 外，金 融 資 產 及 金 融 負 債
        15”). Transaction costs that are directly attributable              初 步 按 公 平 值 計 量。收 購 或 發
        to the acquisition or issue of financial assets and                 行金融資產及金融負債直接應
        financial liabilities are added to or deducted from                 佔之交易成本乃於初始確認時
        the fair value of the financial assets or financial                 加入或扣除自金融資產或金融
        liabilities, as appropriate, on initial recognition.                負債之公平值（按情況而定）。

        The effective interest method is a method of                        實際利率法乃計算金融資產或
        calculating the amortised cost of a financial asset                 金融負債之攤銷成本及於有關
        or financial liability and of allocating interest                   期間分配利息收入及利息開支
        income and interest expense over the relevant                       之 方 法。實 際 利 率 為 於 金 融 資
        period. The effective interest rate is the rate that                產 或 金 融 負 債 預 計 年 期（或 適
        exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts                    用 之 較 短 期 間）內 準 確 地 貼 現
        and payments (including all fees and points                         估 計 未 來 現 金 收 入 及 付 款（包
        paid or received that form an integral part of the                  括構成實際利率一部分之一切
        effective interest rate, transaction costs and other                已 付 或 已 收 費 用 及 點 數、交 易
        premiums or discounts) through the expected life                    成 本 及 其 他 溢 價 或 折 扣）至 其
        of the financial asset or financial liability, or, where            初步確認時之賬面淨值之利率。
        appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying
        amount on initial recognition.

                The Group derecognises a financial asset only                  本集團僅於從資產收取現金流
                when the contractual rights to the cash flows from             量 的 合 約 權 利 屆 滿 時，或 其 轉

                asset and substantially all the risks and rewards of           絕大部分風險及回報轉讓予另
                ownership of the asset to another entity.                      一 實 體 時，本 集 團 方 會 終 止 確

                carrying amount and the sum of the consideration               及應收代價總和之間的差額於
                received and receivable is recognised in profit or             損益確認。
                loss.

                instrument classified as at FVTOCI, the cumulative             面 收 益 的 債 務 工 具 投 資 時，先
                gain or loss previously accumulated in reserve is              前累積於儲備中的累計收益或
                reclassified to profit or loss.                                虧損重新分類至損益。

                deducting all of its liabilities. Equity instruments           權 益 之 任 何 合 約。本 集 團 發 行

                received, net of direct issue costs.                           除直接發行成本）時確認。

                Financial liabilities at amortised cost                        按攤銷成本列賬之金融負債

        paid and payable is recognised in profit or loss.

        and the net amount presented in the consolidated                  可執行權利抵銷已確認金額及
        statement of financial position when, and only when,              擬按淨額基準結算或同時變現
        the Group currently has a legally enforceable right to            資 產 及 結 算 負 債 時，金 融 資 產
        set off the recognised amounts; and intends either                及 金 融 負 債 可 互 相 抵 銷，並 在
        to settle on a net basis, or to realise the asset and             綜合財務狀況表內呈列淨額。

      4    CRITICAL ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENT                                   4   關鍵會計判斷和估計不確
           AND KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION                                       定性的關鍵來源
           UNCERTAINTY
           In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, the          於 應 用 本 集 團 之 會 計 政 策 時，董 事
           Directors are required to make judgements, estimates and            須對未能透過其他來源確定之資產
           assumptions about the carrying amounts of assets and                及 負 債 之 賬 面 值 作 出 判 斷、估 計 及
           liabilities that are not readily apparent from other sources.       假 設。估 計 及 相 關 假 設 乃 根 據 過 往
           The estimates and underlying assumptions are based on               經驗及其他被視為相關之因素作出。
           historical experience and other factors that are considered         實際結果可能與該等估計不同。
           to be relevant. Actual results may differ from these
           estimates.

           The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed               本集團會持續地對上述估計和相關
           on an on-going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates             假 設 進 行 覆 核。如 果 會 計 估 計 的 變
           are recognised in the period in which the estimate is               更 僅 對 變 更 的 當 期 構 成 影 響，則 在
           revised if the revision affects only that period, or in the         變 更 的 當 期 予 以 確 認，或 者 如 果 變
           period of the revision and future periods if the revision           更 對 當 期 和 未 來 期 間 均 構 成 影 響，
           affects both current and future periods.                            則同時在變更的當期和未來期間內
                                                                               予以確認。

           Critical judgements in applying accounting                          應用會計政策的關鍵判斷
           policies

           policies and that have the most significant effect on               認之款項造成最重大影響的重大判
           the amounts recognised in the consolidated financial                斷。
           statements.

    Allowance for credit loss           信貸虧損撥備                             (22,566)            (34,533)
                                                                           339,410             369,973


   The Group gives credit term to key customers, which                本 集 團 授 予 關 鍵 客 戶 信 貸 期，信 貸
   granted with credit term ranging from 7 to 90 days upon            期為交貨後的7至90天。而對於其他
   delivery. While for other customers, payment in advance            客 戶，則 通 常 須 預 收 款 項 或 於 交 貨
   or payment on delivery is normally required. The Group             時 收 款。本 集 團 尋 求 維 持 嚴 格 控 制
   seeks to maintain strict control over its outstanding              其未償還應收賬款及設有信貸控制
   receivables and has credit control commissioners to                專 員，務 求 將 信 貸 風 險 減 至 最 低。
   minimise credit risk. Overdue balances are reviewed                高 級 管 理 層 定 期 審 核 逾 期 結 餘。鑒
   regularly by senior management. In view of the fact that           於本集團涉及大量不同客戶之應收

   The Group’s employees in Hong Kong participates in                本集團於香港的僱員參與根據職業

   Kong. The assets of the schemes were held separately              人 控 制 之 基 金 持 有，與 本 集 團 之 資
   from those of the Group, in funds under the control               產 分 開 處 理。強 制 性 公 積 金 計 劃 供
   of trustees. Contributions to the MPF Scheme were                 款乃根據強制性公積金條例規定的
   in accordance with the statutory limits prescribed by             法 定 限 額 作 出。倘 僱 員 於 獲 得 全 數
   the MPF Ordinance. Where there were employees who                 供款前退出職業退休計劃條例計劃，
   leave the ORSO Scheme prior to vesting fully in the               則本集團應付供款被沒收供款之金

      36 CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                          36 資本風險管理
           The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to               本集團管理資本之目標為保障本集
           safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going              團 持 續 經 營 之 能 力，以 為 股 東 帶 來
           concern in order to provide returns for shareholders and          回報及為其他權益持有人帶來利益，
           benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain an optimal        以及維持最佳資本結構以減低資本
           capital structure to reduce the cost of capital. The Group’s      成 本。本 集 團 的 整 體 策 略 與 去 年 相
           overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.               比保持不變。

           In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure,             為 維 持 或 調 整 資 本 結 構，本 集 團 可
           the Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid to              能 會 調 整 支 付 予 股 東 的 股 息 金 額、
           shareholders, provide returns for shareholders, issue new         為 股 東 提 供 回 報、發 行 新 股 或 出 售
           shares or sell assets.                                            資產。

           The Directors review the capital structure on regular             董 事 定 期 檢 討 資 本 結 構。作 為 此 次
           basis. As part of this review, the Directors consider the         檢 討 的 一 部 分，董 事 會 考 慮 資 本 成
           cost of capital and the risks associated with each class of       本 及 與 各 類 資 本 相 關 的 風 險。根 據
           capital. Based on recommendations of the Directors, the           董 事 的 建 議，本 集 團 將 透 過 支 付 股
           Group will balance its overall capital structure through the      息、發 行 新 債 務 或 贖 回 現 有 債 務 平
           payment of dividends, issue of new debt or the redemption         衡其整體資本結構。
           of existing debt.

        The Group categorised its financial instruments                  本集團於報告期末按公平值層
        measured at fair value at the end of the reporting               級將其按公平值計量的金融工
        period by the level in the fair value hierarchy as               具分類如下：
        below:

        Level 1: The fair value of financial instruments                  第1級： 於活躍市場買賣之金
                 traded in active markets (such as publicly                    融 工 具（如 公 開 買 賣
                 traded derivatives, and equity securities)                    之衍生工具及股本證
                 is based on quoted market prices at the                       券）之 公 平 值 乃 根 據
                 end of the reporting period. The quoted                       報告期末之市場報價
                 market price used for financial assets held                   計 算。本 集 團 所 持 金
                 by the Group is the current bid price. The                    融資產所用之市場報
                 quoted market price already incorporates                      價 為 當 時 買 入 價。所
                 the market’s assumptions with respect                         報市場價格已考慮市
                 to changes in economic climate such                           場有關經濟環境變動
                 as rising interest rates and inflation, as                    如利率增長和通貨膨
                 well as changes due to ESG risk. These                        脹 等 之 假 設，以 及 因
                 instruments are included in level 1.                          環 境、社 會 及 管 治 風
                                                                               險 引 致 之 變 動。該 等
                                                                               工具計入第1級。

        Level 2: The fair value of financial instruments that             第2級： 並非在活躍市場買賣
                 are not traded in an active market (for                       之 金 融 工 具（例 如 場
                 example, over-the-counter derivatives) is                     外 衍 生 工 具）之 公 平
                 determined using valuation techniques                         值乃使用估值技術釐
                 which maximise the use of observable                          定，以 盡 量 利 用 可 觀
                 market data and rely as little as possible on                 察市場資料及盡量少
                 entity-specific estimates. If all significant                 依 賴 實 體 特 定 估 計。
                 inputs required to fair value an instrument                   倘計算工具公平值所
                 are observable, the instrument is included                    需之所有重大輸入資
                 in level 2.                                                   料 均 為 可 觀 察 資 料，
                                                                               則該工具計入第2級。

                 not based on observable market data, the                      入資料並非根據可觀
                 instrument is included in level 3. This is                    察 市 場 資 料，則 有 關
                 the case for unlisted equity securities and                   工 具 計 入 第3級。就

                 to a significant unobservable adjustment.                     境、社 會 及 管 治 風 險
                                                                               導致重大不可觀察調

                                                                               就是如此。
